namespace = cosmo_concubineestate_events

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_concubineestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_concubineestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_EUROPE_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nobleaffairs_launch		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nobleaffairs_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_nobleaffairs_ruler_visit
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_nobleaffairs_marry_consort
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_harem_ruler_visit_concubines
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_concubineestate_events.10.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_concubineonly_visit
			}
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.101
				}
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_ruler_flag = cosmo_concubineonly_visit
				}
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_isolation_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.1
			}
		}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_concubineonly_visit
	}					
}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.101
	title = cosmo_concubineestate_events.101.t
	desc = cosmo_concubineestate_events.101.d
	picture = cosmogfx_CONCUBINE_BARBARY_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_menu_1_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = barbary_concubine_1a
				has_province_modifier = barbary_concubine_1b
				has_province_modifier = barbary_concubine_1c
			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_eurowives_menu_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_menu_2_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = barbary_concubine_2a

					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = bsea_concubine_2a
						has_province_modifier = bsea_concubine_2b
						has_province_modifier = bsea_concubine_2c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = bsea_concubine_4a
						has_province_modifier = bsea_concubine_4b
						has_province_modifier = bsea_concubine_4c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_1a
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_1b
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_1c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_2a
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_2b
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_2c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_3a
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_3b
						has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_3c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = southsea_concubine_1a
						has_province_modifier = southsea_concubine_1b
						has_province_modifier = southsea_concubine_1c
					}

				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoEuropeanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesFiveCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoConcubinesFiveCCounter	
			value = 460
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesFiveCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoEuropeanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}

		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesEightCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoConcubinesEightCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_navigator
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoConcubinesEightCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesEightCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}

			}
			}
		set_variable = {

	}

		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.101
			}
		}

			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

		}

			}
		}

			}
			}

		which = cosmoConcubinesTwelveACounter

			}
		}
	}

	picture = cosmogfx_CONCUBINE_HORN_MENU_2C_5_eventPicture #2nd
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = horn_concubine_2c
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_concubineestate_events.1230.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoEuropeanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesTwelveCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoConcubinesTwelveCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_theologian
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoConcubinesTwelveCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesTwelveCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}

				which = cosmoEuropeanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesFifteenBCounter
		value = 20
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoConcubinesFifteenBCounter	
			value = 480
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoConcubinesFifteenBCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}

			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.101
			}
		}

			limit = {
					any_province = {

		}		
		}

			id = cosmo_concubineestate_events.1610
			}
		}
		}

		name = cosmo_concubineestate_events.1600.b
